Abstract: We present an architecture that enables data owners to combine private data into data pools using Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) and manage these pools by issuing narrowly scoped computational rights, encoded as Digital Rights Tokens (DRTs), to third-party data analysts. Each DRT binds specific open-source code to a pool and is issued and redeemed on a distributed ledger. Data analysts can obtain the right to execute open-source code on the combined sealed data inside a TEE and receive the result from this code execution, but not the underlying data. We argue for a control-centric view of privacy in which creators retain ex ante control over how their data is processed. A reference implementation runs WebAssembly (WASM)/Python jobs over sealed datasets and records redemptions on Solana, illustrating the feasibility and limitations of the platform.

</details>


### [214] [CASCADE Against Jailbreaks: Combination Across Stages with Controlled Attack-Defense Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21793)
*Jiale Luo,Eric Han*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首个系统性研究LLM越狱防御的组合策略，在统一威胁模型下评测19种攻击与15种防御，发现单一防御并非普适最优，但合理组合可在几乎不损失效用的前提下显著提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对LLM越狱攻击的防御分布在不同流水线阶段（如输入修改、输出过滤），但各阶段该部署何种防御、如何组合仍不明确；已有实证研究因攻击成功率定义与实验设置不一致而各自孤立，缺乏可比性。

Method: 在直接、黑盒、单轮攻击的统一威胁模型下，提出决策框架：以原则化的攻击成功率公式与受控查询预算标准化评测，并设定明确的公平性规则；覆盖19种攻击与15种防御，系统比较防御在阶段内与跨阶段的组合效果。

Result: 没有任何单一防御在所有情形下表现最优；经过良好选择的防御组合能以极小的效用损失换取大幅安全性提升，并据此给出分层防御流水线的实用建议。

Conclusion: 防御应作为分层流水线整体设计，组合优于孤立部署；该决策框架为跨阶段防御的标准化评测与选型提供了可复用基准。

Abstract: Defenses against jailbreak attacks on Large Language Models (LLMs) operate at different pipeline stages, such as input modification or output guard, but it remains unclear which defenses to deploy at each stage and how to combine them. Prior empirical studies, fragmented by inconsistent attack-success-rate definitions and experimental settings, have evaluated defenses largely in isolation. Here we present the first systematic study, to our knowledge, of defense combinations both within and across pipeline stages, under a consistent threat model of direct, black-box, single-turn attacks. Our decision framework standardizes evaluation through a principled attack-success-rate formulation with controlled query budgets, together with explicit fairness rules. Across 19 attacks and 15 defenses, we find that no single defense is universally best, but well-chosen combinations achieve substantial safety with minimal utility degradation, yielding practical recommendations for layered defense pipelines.

</details>


### [215] [Watermarkable Multi-Draft Speculative Sampling via Poisson Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21858)
*Yanxiao Liu,Sicheng Wan,Zhan Gao,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于泊松过程的多草稿推测采样算法，可同时兼顾采样效率与无偏水印嵌入，并具备草稿器不变性，是首个兼具水印强度与采样效率的多草稿、草稿器不变推测采样方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署需要兼顾推理效率与输出溯源，通常分别通过推测采样与加水印实现。但已有研究表明二者结合极为困难甚至可能无法兼得，本文旨在改善这一权衡的前沿。

Method: 提出基于泊松过程的新型多草稿（multi-draft）推测采样算法，可在不降低推测接受率的前提下嵌入无偏水印；算法建立在精确的无通信列表耦合（list-coupling-without-communication）方案之上，从而获得草稿器不变（drafter invariance）性质。

Result: 该算法自身具有强采样效率，且天然可水印化；其草稿器不变性同时有利于采样与水印；实验验证了其在水印强度与采样效率两方面均有强劲表现。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了多草稿、草稿器不变的推测采样方案，能同时保持水印强度与采样效率，改善了效率与溯源之间的根本性权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved state-of-the-art performance across a wide range of tasks, motivating two important aspects of deployment: inference efficiency and output provenance, which can be tackled by speculative sampling and watermarking, respectively. However, recent works have shown that combining these two goals is highly nontrivial and can be potentially impossible. In this work, we develop a novel multi-draft speculative sampling algorithm based on Poisson processes that improves the frontier of this fundamental trade-off. The proposed algorithm has strong sampling efficiency on its own and, more interestingly, is naturally watermarkable: we can embed an unbiased watermark without degrading speculative acceptance. Moreover, our algorithm is based on an exact list-coupling-without-communication scheme, which yields a drafter invariance property that benefits both sampling and watermarking. It is the first multi-draft, drafter-invariant speculative sampling scheme that maintains both watermark strength and sampling efficiency, and we experimentally verify its strong performance in both aspects.

</details>


### [216] [SFPF: Spatio-Frequency Polarization Fingerprint for Anomalous Wireless Device Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21873)
*Xiaoxuan Huang,Jinlong Xu,Daoyuan Shen,Meng Zhang,Dong Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出时空-频率极化指纹（SFPF），通过联合建模多个频率和观测方向上的复极化响应来检测被替换的无线设备，超越传统射频指纹（RFF）和单方向极化指纹（PF）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署无线设备的定期检查十分必要，因为未经授权的硬件替换可能保留通信功能、凭证和逻辑身份，使异常设备难以被发现。传统RFF在替换硬件与合法硬件高度相似时区分度不足，而单一观测方向构建的PF可能遗漏空间非均匀的极化变化。

Method: 提出SFPF，联合表示多个频率和观测方向上的复极化响应，传统PF是其固定方向切片。从硬件相关的模态激励、方向性远场辐射和极化投影推导SFPF的形成过程。通过一阶灵敏度分析表明同一硬件变化的响应随频率和方向变化，从而支持联合时空-频率采集。

Result: 电磁仿真验证了非均匀的时空-频率灵敏度，在相同观测预算下，SFPF相比PF在归一化距离、Fisher分数和类间/类内比上分别提升17.7%、45.8%和11.3%。实验表明SFPF在0--20 dB范围内持续优于RFF和PF。在15--20 dB时，SFPF实现异常设备F1分数87.3--90.4%，AUROC值85.4--95.5%。

Conclusion: 联合时空-频率极化指纹能有效提升硬件替换检测性能，优于传统RFF和PF方法。

Abstract: Periodic inspection of deployed wireless devices is necessary because unauthorized hardware replacement may preserve communication functions, credentials, and logical identity, making anomalous devices difficult to detect. Such inspections are conducted under controlled measurement conditions to verify that each device remains consistent with its enrolled hardware state. Conventional radio-frequency fingerprint (RFF) may provide insufficient separation when replacement hardware closely resembles legitimate hardware, while a polarization fingerprint (PF) constructed at one observation direction may miss spatially nonuniform polarization changes. This paper proposes the spatio-frequency polarization fingerprint (SFPF), which jointly represents complex polarization responses over multiple frequencies and observation directions; conventional PF is its fixed-direction slice. We derive SFPF formation from hardware-dependent modal excitation, directional far-field radiation, and polarization projection. A first-order sensitivity analysis shows that the response to the same hardware change varies with both frequency and direction, motivating joint spatio-frequency acquisition. Electromagnetic simulations confirm the nonuniform spatio-frequency sensitivity and show that, under the same observation budget, SFPF improves normalized distance, Fisher score, and the inter-/intra-class ratio over PF by 17.7%, 45.8%, and 11.3%, respectively. Experiments show that SFPF consistently outperforms RFF and PF over 0--20~dB. At 15--20~dB, SFPF achieves anomalous-device F1 scores of 87.3--90.4% and AUROC values of 85.4--95.5%.

</details>


### [217] [End-to-End Hard-Label Cryptanalytic Model Extraction Using Efficient Sign Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21941)
*Akira Ito,Takayuki Miura,Yosuke Todo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的符号恢复算法，无需专用查询，实现硬标签黑盒端到端模型提取，在MNIST等数据集上达到98%以上标签一致率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN参数是重要资产，模型提取攻击备受关注。硬标签设置最具挑战性，但现有方法（Carlini et al., Eurocrypt 2025）的符号恢复步骤需要大量查询和计算，难以在黑盒下实现，端到端演示仍未被完成。

Method: 提出基于全新原理的符号恢复算法，不依赖专用查询，且不需要针对符号恢复的额外查询设计，从而将硬标签模型提取的所有步骤在完全黑盒设置下实现。

Result: 新算法符号恢复准确率高于现有方法，可高效处理训练后模型。在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上，对宽度16、4或6隐藏层的模型实现端到端提取，标签一致率超过98%。

Conclusion: 该工作使硬标签模型提取的全部步骤可在黑盒下执行，验证了端到端黑盒提取的可行性。

Abstract: The importance of deep neural networks (DNNs) is widely recognized, and the parameters obtained through training are regarded as valuable assets. Recently, attacks that extract these parameters using only oracle queries to a DNN have been actively studied at IACR conferences. The hard-label setting is the most challenging setting for model extraction, where an adversary can observe only the final output label, such as "dog" or "cat." At Eurocrypt 2025, Carlini et al. proposed polynomial-time hard-label extraction of ReLU-based MLPs. However, one step of this attack process, i.e., sign recovery, requires a large number of queries and substantial computation. Implementing this step in a black-box setting remains difficult. Consequently, a fully black-box end-to-end demonstration on trained deep ReLU MLPs has remained a challenge. In this paper, we propose a new sign-recovery algorithm based on a completely different principle from the existing method. Our method requires no dedicated queries for sign recovery. In our experiments, it achieves higher sign-recovery accuracy than the existing method. Consequently, it enables efficient sign recovery even for trained models. With our sign-recovery algorithm, all steps of hard-label model extraction can be implemented in a black-box setting. By combining these implementations, we demonstrate end-to-end model extraction from models trained on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST, with width 16 and 4 or 6 hidden layers, achieving over 98% label agreement.

</details>


### [218] [Provisional Reachability: Containing Agents by Making Every Crossing Revocable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21957)
*Yoshiaki Takashita*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于托管与概率审计的防御方案，将"每周期比特数"这一单位正式设定下来，用审计率控制对手可获取的信息量，并通过"秘密衰减"机制使长秘密不可达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作指出防御者随时间必须阻断的量有单位——每周期比特（bits per period），本文旨在具体设定并实现这一速率。目标是让秘密无法在单次运行中聚合，并对抗随时间累积的信息泄漏。

Method: 将所有跨越项托管一个周期，以概率 r 独立审计每个滞留项，若审计发现问题则撤销窗口。对手携带 c 比特穿越 k 次，期望信息量 kc(1-r)^k，在 k*=1/ln(1/(1-r)) 处最大，给出每窗口界 L(r)~c/(er)。此外引入秘密衰减机制：滞留比特每周期以比例 mu 衰减，使持有量收敛至 g/mu，当 mu > g/L 时 L 比特秘密不可达。还测试了欺骗、保留参考、密钥化入口点与指示符等手段。同时分析了方差在审计率与激活预算中的不同处置效应。

Result: 仿真与理论界吻合至 7.7 个标准误；误差阈值锐利（0.9mu* 时 100% 运行可聚合，2mu* 时 0%）。欺骗性手段失败（18/18 准确率维持）。密钥化入口点隐藏 0.10 比特，密钥化指示符隐藏 2.64/3.00 比特且 100% 读者仍视其为普通 Python。移除审计率方差使灭绝率从 70% 升至 100%。端到端将泄漏从 100,000 比特降至 59 比特（因子 1,704），保留 12% 合法工作；将窗口密钥化至调用者可在零泄漏代价下恢复至 100%。对 65 个只读工具，每次调用仍剩 2,400 比特（因子 10，而非无穷）。

Conclusion: 托管加概率审计给出的是速率界而非总量界，单靠托管仍让秘密在每次运行中聚合；引入衰减后产生 Eigen 意义上的尖锐误差阈值。密钥化窗口至调用者可在不增加泄漏的前提下恢复全部合法工作，但代价是界变为按主体计算。该防御栈显著压缩泄漏，但无法在有限工具集上实现完全阻断。

Abstract: A companion paper found that what a defender must block over time has units: bits per period [Takashita, 2026a]. This paper sets it. Hold every crossing in escrow for one period, audit each held item independently with probability r, and revoke the window if any audit catches something. An adversary crossing k times, each carrying c bits, expects kc(1-r)^k, maximised at k* = 1/ln(1/(1-r)), a bound of L(r) ~ c/(er) per window. The bound is a supremum over the adversary's choice, so the scheme may be public; simulation matches it to 7.7 standard errors. It is a rate, not a total: escrow alone still lets the secret assemble in every run. But if the secret decays at a fraction mu of held bits per period, holdings converge to g/mu at any horizon, so an L-bit secret is unreachable once mu > g/L -- an error threshold in Eigen's sense, sharp where the closed form puts it (100% of runs assemble at 0.9mu*, 0% at 2mu*, over 20,000 windows). Deception that needs the adversary to reason badly fails: a surface whose names lie left accuracy at 18 of 18, and 100% at three reader strengths. Withholding reference works, and differently: no reader would commit at all. Keying the entry points hides 0.10 bits of what a module does; keying the denotation hides 2.64 of 3.00 at chance accuracy, while 100% of readers still call it ordinary Python. Variance must be removed from the audit rate, where loot is convex in r, and added to the activation budget, where survival is multiplicative: extinction 70% to 100% at a fixed mean. End to end the stack takes the leak from 100,000 to 59 bits, a factor of 1,704, leaving 12% of legitimate work standing; keying the window to the caller restores that to 100% at no cost in leakage, at the price of a bound that is per principal. Of 65 read-only tools, escrow leaves 2,400 bits per call: a factor of 10, not infinity.

</details>


### [219] [The Supersingular Isogeny Problem in Time and Memory $p^{1/3+o(1)}$, Unconditionally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22018)
*José Luis Delgado*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文给出求解超奇异椭圆曲线 $\mathsf{OneEnd}$ 问题的 Las Vegas 算法，期望时间与内存为 $p^{1/3+o(1)}$，且不依赖光滑性启发式假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathsf{OneEnd}$ 问题可归约到超奇异自同态环问题与同源问题。此前无条件复杂度为指数 $2/5$，Wesolowski 在小度数因式分解假设下达到 $1/3$。本文希望去掉该假设，给出无需光滑性启发式的无条件 $1/3$ 算法。

Method: 预先固定一族由小素数乘积构成的度数。利用已知计数结果得到从曲线到其 Frobenius 共轭曲线的大量该度数同源，并通过碰撞估计证明这些同源出现在足够多不同曲线上，使随机游走能到达其中之一。到达后将该度数拆成两部分，枚举两组更短同源并匹配目标，得到到共轭的同源，再与 Frobenius 复合得到所需自同态。

Result: 算法为 Las Vegas 型，在不使用光滑性启发式分析的情况下，期望时间与内存为 $p^{1/3}\exp(O(\sqrt{\log p\,\log\log p}))=p^{1/3+o(1)}$。

Conclusion: 该工作将 $\mathsf{OneEnd}$ 问题的无条件复杂度从指数 $2/5$ 改进到 $1/3+o(1)$，并去除了先前对因数分解假设的依赖。

Abstract: Given a supersingular elliptic curve $E/\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$, the $\mathsf{OneEnd}$ problem asks for a non-scalar endomorphism of $E$. By known reductions, solving this problem also solves the supersingular endomorphism ring and isogeny problems. Wesolowski obtained exponent $1/3$ under an assumption on the factorization of a small degree, whereas the previous unconditional exponent was $2/5$. We give a Las Vegas algorithm, analyzed without a smoothness heuristic, with expected time and memory \[ p^{1/3}\exp\bigl(O(\sqrt{\log p\,\log\log p})\bigr) = p^{1/3+o(1)}. \]
  The algorithm fixes in advance a family of degrees that are products of small primes. Known counting results provide many isogenies of these degrees from curves to their Frobenius conjugates, and a collision estimate shows that the isogenies occur on sufficiently many distinct curves for a random walk to reach one of them. From such a curve, the algorithm splits a degree into two parts, enumerates two lists of shorter isogenies, and matches their targets to obtain an isogeny to the conjugate, whose composition with Frobenius gives the required endomorphism.

</details>


### [220] [APort Vault: Benchmarking AI Agent Payment Authorization with the Open Agent Passport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22076)
*Uchi Uchibeke*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: APort Vault 是一个针对"工具使用型 AI 智能体"支付授权能力的基准测试，通过公开夺旗活动中人类编写的 4,371 个攻击，对 8 家机构 14 个模型、5 种策略配置、2 条重放轨道进行 225,964 次评测，并在有无 OAP 规范确定性预动作检查两种条件下对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准往往把多个评测事件压缩成单一数字，导致结论经不起审查；同时支付授权场景缺乏真实人类攻击驱动、覆盖多种策略配置的评测。该工作旨在建立细粒度、可复现的支付授权基准。

Method: 重放夺旗活动中人类对在线支付智能体的攻击，覆盖 14 个模型、5 种策略配置和 2 条重放轨道；引入实现 Open Agent Passport (OAP) 规范的确定性预动作检查作为对照。每次评测报告五个独立事件而非折叠为单一指标。分析包含全模型共同的 Level 4 提示集、跨层级授权边界对比，以及模型/提示/轨道三重匹配的配对比较与每会话上界估计。

Result: 请求率随配置差异远大于随模型差异：Level 1 为 10.9%、Level 2 为 3.0%、Level 3 为 0.1%、Level 4 为 79.4%。Level 4 的 1,293 个提示在各模型上请求率为 71.2%–84.3%，其中 809 个（62.6%）被全部 14 个模型请求并成功支付给白名单收款人。授权边界差异显著：Level 2–4 中向非许可收款人的转账，纯模型为 140/76,842，加层后为 0/69,297；三重匹配比较为 105 对 0。该零值覆盖 790 个源会话，每会话上界 0.38%。加层后仍执行 25,370 笔支付，策略评估 25,640 次转账调用中拒绝 187 次，其中 148 次为禁止收款人。

Conclusion: 确定性预动作检查层能在不拒绝合法支付的前提下，将非许可收款人转账降至零，且模型间的授权边界差异主要体现在条件而非请求率上。作者公开数据集、层级护照、评分代码与分析脚本以支持复现。

Abstract: APort Vault is a benchmark for payment authorization in tool-using AI agents. It replays 4,371 attacks written by humans against a live payment agent during a public capture-the-flag event, across 14 models from 8 labs, five policy configurations and two replay tracks, with and without a deterministic pre-action check implementing the Open Agent Passport (OAP) specification. 225,964 evaluations completed. We report five distinct events per evaluation, because collapsing them is how an agent benchmark produces a number that does not survive review.
  Requests are common and their rate differs far more across configurations than across models, though each attack exists at exactly one configuration so policy and attack cohort vary together: 10.9% of model-alone evaluations at Level 1, 3.0% at Level 2, 0.1% at Level 3, 79.4% at Level 4. On the 1,293 Level 4 prompts, each evaluated on every model, request rates run from 71.2% to 84.3%, and 809 prompts (62.6%) elicited a request from all fourteen models, each ending in a successful payment to the level's allowlisted recipient.
  The authorization boundary is where the conditions diverge. At Levels 2 to 4, transfers to recipients the passport did not permit number 140 of 76,842 with the model alone and 0 of 69,297 behind the layer, and 105 against 0 on 68,970 matched model, prompt and track triples. The zero spans 790 source sessions, giving a per-session upper bound of 0.38%. It was not obtained by refusing payments: 25,370 payments executed behind the layer, while the policy denied 187 of the 25,640 transfer calls it evaluated, 148 of them for a forbidden recipient.
  We release the 225,964 evaluations, the level passports, the scoring code and the analysis script at huggingface.co/datasets/aporthq/vault-benchmark-v1 .

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [BirdsongChat: A Hybrid Multi-Agent Framework for Multimodal Embodied Behavior Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20887)
*Callie C. Liao,Duoduo Liao,Ellie L. Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出混合多智能体框架，用统一参数表示（UPR）连接语义推理与物理执行，并以鸟类行为模拟原型BirdsongChat验证其跨模态一致性与可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态智能体多依赖隐式表示，导致可控性差、跨模态一致性不足，难以将人类意图转化为可解释、协调的多模态行为。

Method: 构建混合多智能体框架：LLM推理智能体将多模态输入转为统一参数表示（UPR），显式编码行为状态与可解释控制参数，再由仿真智能体生成同步的3D运动、空间化声景与环境行为；以鸟类交互模拟原型BirdsongChat作为测试平台。

Result: 在文本与图像引导的物种、行为、情感状态、环境及多鸟交互场景中评估，跨模态连贯性归一化得分94.4%，情感一致性100%，生成一致性92.6%。

Conclusion: 显式中间表示能有效桥接语义推理与物理执行，提升可控性与多模态同步，为具身AI提供可推广的设计原则，可应用于生物启发生态声学、集群机器人、虚拟环境与创意多媒体。

Abstract: Multimodal embodied systems require translating human intentions into interpretable and coordinated behaviors across heterogeneous modalities. However, existing multimodal agents often rely on implicit representations, limiting controllability and cross-modal consistency. We present a hybrid multi-agent framework for interactive multimodal behavior simulation that bridges semantic reasoning and physical execution through a Unified Parameter Representation (UPR). LLM-based reasoning agents transform multimodal inputs into UPR, which encodes behavioral states and interpretable control parameters for simulation agents generating synchronized 3D motion, spatialized soundscapes, and environmental behaviors. We develop BirdsongChat as a prototype implementation of the proposed framework, using interactive avian behavior simulation as a testbed that tightly couples motion, vocalization, and environmental context. BirdsongChat is evaluated on text- and image-guided scenarios involving species, behaviors, affective states, environments, and multi-bird interactions. The system achieves normalized scores of 94.4\% for cross-modal coherence, 100% for affective consistency, and 92.6% for generation consistency. These results demonstrate that an explicit intermediate representation effectively bridges semantic reasoning and physical execution, improving controllability and multimodal synchronization. The proposed framework thus offers a generalizable design principle for embodied AI systems requiring interpretable semantic-to-physical coordination across modalities, with potential applications in bio-inspired ecoacoustics, swarm robotics, virtual environments, and creative multimedia.

</details>


### [222] [Proxifield: Decentralized Multi-Agent Communication through Semantic Proximity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20889)
*Pradyumna Tambwekar,Yenchia Feng,Deep Patel,Karime Maamari*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出 Proxifield——一种轮次自适应、去中心化的多智能体通信协议，通过智能体语义邻近度构建稀疏通信图，在可扩展性与容错性上显著优于中心化 Star 与去中心化 Shared Context 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 能力扩展，多智能体通信成为活跃研究方向。但现有协议多采用刚性结构，会引入协调瓶颈，并随智能体数量增加而性能退化。因此需要一种去中心化、可自适应、可扩展的通信协议。

Method: 提出 Proxifield：轮次自适应的多智能体协议，去中心化决策，无需模型训练与中心化规划器。基于智能体不断演化的语义邻近度构建稀疏通信图，在推理时依据四类路由信号连接智能体：直接寻址、信息需求、计划对齐、信息互补。在 Drone Search and Rescue 与 HiddenBench 两个领域，与中心化 Star 和去中心化 Shared Context 两种基线进行对比，并做基座模型能力消融。

Result: 在两个领域中，Proxifield 性能随模型规模提升（35B→397B）而改善，并在最大规模下超越所有基线。随团队规模增大，Proxifield 相对 Star 的任务奖励优势从 N=5 时的 5.4% 扩大到 N=25 时的 53.0%、N=50 时的 59.5%；Shared Context 始终表现最差。在永久性智能体故障下，Proxifield 在最严重条件仍保留 73.6% 的无故障任务奖励，高于 Shared Context 的 58.3% 与 Star 的 38.8%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，去中心化、语义自适应的路由能够提升多智能体系统的可扩展性与容错能力。

Abstract: As LLM capabilities have expanded, multi-agent communication has emerged as an increasingly active area of research. Prevailing protocols often adopt rigid structures that introduce coordination bottlenecks and can degrade as the number of agents increases. We introduce Proxifield, a round-adaptive multi-agent protocol with decentralized agent decision-making that constructs sparse communication graphs from the evolving semantic proximity of agents. Without model training or a centralized planner, Proxifield connects agents using four routing signals derived at inference time: direct address, information needs, plan alignment, and information complementarity. We compare Proxifield with two representative coordination baselines, a centralized Star protocol and a decentralized Shared Context protocol, across two domains: Drone Search and Rescue and the collective-reasoning benchmark HiddenBench. We first ablate base-model capability and find that, in both domains, the performance of Proxifield improves with model size (35B -> 397B parameter model) and Proxifield outperforms all baselines at the largest scale. As team size increases, Proxifield's task-reward advantage over Star widens from 5.4% at (N=5) to 53.0% at (N=25) and 59.5% at (N=50), while Shared Context consistently underperforms both protocols. Proxifield is also substantially more robust to permanent agent failure, retaining 73.6% of its no-failure task reward under the most severe condition, compared with 58.3% for Shared Context and 38.8% for Star. These results demonstrate that decentralized, semantically adaptive routing can improve the scalability and fault tolerance of multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [223] [CityLearn v3: A Configurable Simulation and Evaluation Framework for Realistic Control Studies of Renewable Energy Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21570)
*Tiago Fonseca,Luis Lino Ferreira,Armando Sousa,Ava Mohammadi,Zoltan Nagy*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出CityLearn v3，一个可配置的仿真与评估框架，用于在成员与资产变化、设备可用性、服务截止时间和数据质量不确定等条件下研究可再生能源社区（RECs）控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有REC控制器研究常简化处理参与变化、设备可用性、服务截止时间和数据质量，导致较低成本或削峰结果可能掩盖未交付服务或不可行的功率请求。

Method: 在单一仿真环境中表示变化的成员与资产、柔性负荷截止时间、需求响应请求、本地能量共享以及数据或设备故障；用建筑与相位功率限制约束可控请求，并以声明的时间步长保证功率-能量核算一致；记录控制器输入，区分请求动作与实际作用于设备的动作；提供参考控制器、服务与约束感知的性能指标及轨迹导出。

Result: 通过软件检查和应用示例考察服务交付、电气约束、结算与场景变化；合成高频轨迹回放显示聚合会掩盖短时峰值而不改变年度能量。

Conclusion: 这些记录使聚合性能可与服务失败、动作削减及参与方层面结果一并解读。

Abstract: Renewable energy communities (RECs) coordinate buildings, photovoltaic generation, batteries, electric vehicles and flexible loads. Controller studies often simplify changing participation, equipment availability, service deadlines and data quality, so lower cost or peak demand can conceal missed services or infeasible power requests. This paper presents CityLearn v3, a configurable simulation and evaluation framework for REC control studies under these conditions. It represents changing members and assets, flexible-load deadlines, demand-response requests, local energy sharing, and data or equipment failures within one simulation environment. Building and phase power limits constrain controllable requests, while a declared timestep preserves consistent power-to-energy accounting. The framework records controller inputs and distinguishes requested actions from those applied to the simulated equipment. Reference controllers, service- and constraint-aware performance indicators, and trajectory exports support comparisons within and across communities. Software checks and application examples examine service delivery, electrical constraints, settlement and changing scenarios; a synthetic high-frequency trace replay illustrates how aggregation can conceal short peaks without changing annual energy. Together, these records allow aggregate performance to be interpreted alongside service failures, action reductions and participant-level outcomes.

</details>


### [224] [Bayesian Belief Layer for Controllable Opinion Dynamics in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21997)
*Hafsa Akbar,Daniel Platnick,Marjan Alirezaie,Hossein Rahnama*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯编年史智能体（BCA），将智能体的"信念内容"与"表达方式"解耦，以单一先验强度参数κ控制固执度，可即时复现共识、持续分歧与坚定少数影响三种舆论动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM社会模拟中观点更新是隐式的、依赖上下文，说服开放性无法指定也无法验证，集体结果直接继承模型训练先验，缺乏可控性与可审计性。

Method: BCA引入一个最小信念层，每句听到的话语触发一次贝叶斯更新，立场用概率表示；用单一先验强度参数κ（仿照Friedkin-Johnsen模型）编码固执程度，并扫描κ以产生三种典型舆论机制。

Result: 持续分歧情形与FJ闭式不动点高度吻合（R²=0.93–0.99）；规定的κ在语言往返转换后仍可恢复，四个模型均实现完美的秩序恢复；显式信念层还能揭示被端到端模拟掩盖的模型特定立场偏差。

Conclusion: 显式贝叶斯信念层使LLM社会模拟变得可控、可验证、可审计，同时保持与经典舆论动力学模型的一致性。

Abstract: LLM agents in social simulation revise their opinions implicitly, in context: how open an agent is to persuasion can neither be specified nor verified, and collective outcomes inherit the model's training prior. We introduce Bayesian Chronicle Agents (BCA), a minimal belief layer separating \emph{what} an agent believes from \emph{how} it speaks. Each stance is a probability, updated by one Bayesian step per utterance heard. A single prior-strength parameter $κ$ encodes stubbornness, modeled after its role in Friedkin--Johnsen (FJ) opinion dynamics. We then sweep this parameter to yield three canonical regimes of opinion dynamics on demand (consensus, persistent disagreement, committed-minority influence), with persistent disagreement matching the FJ closed-form fixed points at $R^2\!=\!0.93$--$0.99$. We further show that prescribed $κ$ remains recoverable after the language round-trip, with perfect rank-order recovery across all four models. Explicit belief also makes simulation auditable: the layer surfaces systematic per-model stance biases that end-to-end simulation would silently absorb.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Helpful but Fallible: Developer Experiences of AI Tools Under a Coordinated Industrial Roll-out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20977)
*Andreas Bexell,Rushali Gupta,Lo Gullstrand Heander,Emma Söderberg,Per Runeson,Sigrid Eldh,Wei Shi,Konstantin Malysh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文通过对瑞典某大型电信公司AI开发工具协同推广的案例研究，揭示开发者体验及对职业变化的预期，发现管理层与开发者之间存在期望落差，且感知风险是现有接受模型未涵盖的重要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI开发工具正被工业界广泛采用，并寄予提升生产力的厚望，但开发者对这类推广的实际体验研究不足。现有证据主要来自工具评估、生产力指标和问卷调查，缺乏对持续、协同推广过程的质性现场研究，导致组织在投入预算、评估员工和调整实践时依据不完整。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，对瑞典某大型电信公司AI开发工具协同推广进行考察。对三个站点的12名软件专业人员开展半结构化访谈，使用过程编码和主题分析，并以扩展技术接受模型（TAM2）作为事后分析框架进行解读。

Result: 研究在用例、生产力、挫折感和工具局限方面的发现印证了此前的调查研究。此外，访谈揭示了管理层与开发者之间的期望落差，该落差对应TAM2中的主观规范和自愿性构念；同时参与者高度重视感知风险，而TAM2及类似接受模型并未纳入这一因素。AI开发工具呈现为有帮助但易出错的助手，其价值受组织期望、系统规模和开发者技能影响。

Conclusion: AI开发工具是有帮助但易出错的助手，其价值由组织期望、系统规模和开发者技能共同塑造。研究补充了现有接受模型对感知风险考量的不足，并为组织协同推广AI开发工具提供了实践启示。

Abstract: AI-enabled software development tools (AI-devtools) are being industrially adopted under strong expectations of productivity gains, yet developers' experiences of such roll-outs are underexplored. Organizations commit budgets, evaluate staff, and revise practice on a partial picture, since the evidence base is mainly tool evaluations, productivity metrics, and surveys, with few qualitative in-situ accounts of ongoing, coordinated roll-outs. We report a case study of a coordinated roll-out of AI-devtools at a large Swedish telecommunications company, investigating how developers experience the roll-out and how they anticipate their profession will change. We conducted semi-structured interviews with 12 software professionals across three sites, analyzed with process coding and thematic analysis, and interpreted through the extended Technology Acceptance Model (TAM2) as a post-hoc analytical lens. Our findings on use cases, productivity, frustrations, and tool limitations corroborate prior survey work. Beyond corroboration, the interviews surface a management-developer expectation gap that maps onto the TAM2 constructs of subjective norm and voluntariness, and show that participants weigh perceived risk heavily, a factor that TAM2 and similar acceptance models do not represent. AI-devtools emerge as helpful but fallible assistants whose value is shaped by organizational expectations, system scale, and developers' skills.

</details>


### [226] [Embedding Drift in Code Vulnerability Models Under Intended Behaviour-Preserving Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21203)
*Hasti Ghaneshirazi,Tahsin Reza,Ladan Tahvildari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 语义保持的代码编辑会改变冻结代码嵌入的预测结果，导致漏洞检测不稳定；本文基于Big-Vul数据集评估该问题并提出一种仅在训练阶段使用的防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保留程序行为的代码编辑（如删除注释、插入不可达代码、变量重命名、循环重写）本不应改变模型的安全判断，但这些编辑可能使冻结的代码嵌入跨越分类器决策边界，把原本正确检测出的漏洞误判为良性，因此需要研究并缓解这种预测不稳定问题。

Method: 使用Big-Vul数据集中15,000个C/C++函数（7,500对漏洞-修补样本），对漏洞与良性样本施加四类变异（注释删除、不可达代码插入、变量重命名、循环重写）及组合变异；采用冻结的microsoft/codebert-base嵌入配合六种下游分类器评估漏洞预测鲁棒性，并提出一种仅在训练阶段使用的防御方法，将干净表示与变异表示投影得更近，同时保留漏洞类别信息。

Result: 组合变异下，基线漏洞翻转率（VFR）介于35.55%至42.15%；所提防御将平均公共集VFR从22.60%降至11.32%，平均变异准确率从55.33%提升至57.10%。对Logistic Regression，VFR下降17.29个百分点，但良性到漏洞的翻转增加11.95个百分点。

Conclusion: 该防御提升了模型对语义保持代码变换的鲁棒性，但引入了假阳性权衡，且未能完全消除预测不稳定性。

Abstract: Code edits that preserve program behaviour can shift frozen code embeddings across classifier decision boundaries, causing correctly detected vulnerabilities to be predicted as benign. Such instability is problematic because semantically neutral changes, including comment removal, insertion of unreachable code, variable renaming, and loop rewriting, should not alter a model's security judgment. We investigate this issue using 15,000 C/C++ functions organized as 7,500 vulnerable-patched pairs from the Big-Vul dataset. Four mutation tracks are applied to both vulnerable and benign samples: comment removal, unreachable code insertion, variable renaming, loop rewriting, and a combined setting that applies all transformations. Using frozen microsoft/codebert-base embeddings with six downstream classifiers, we evaluate the robustness of vulnerability predictions and introduce a train-only defence that projects clean and mutated representations closer together while preserving vulnerability-class information. Under combined mutations, the baseline Vulnerable Flip Rate (VFR) ranges from 35.55% to 42.15%. The proposed defence reduces the mean common-set VFR from 22.60% to 11.32% and improves mean mutated accuracy from 55.33% to 57.10%. For Logistic Regression, VFR decreases by 17.29 percentage points, although benign-to-vulnerable flips increase by 11.95 percentage points. These results indicate that the defence improves robustness to semantics-preserving code transformations but introduces a false-positive trade-off and does not fully eliminate prediction instability.

</details>


### [227] [License Compliance in Open Source Cybersecurity Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21218)
*Ahmed Shah,Selman Selman,Ibrahim Abualhaol*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对200多个开源网络安全项目进行初步分析，发现宽松许可项目中存在限制性许可污染，且大量代码缺少版权归属信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全软件开发商常将开源软件包集成到商业产品中，需确认其许可证是否宽松以支持商业继承与再分发。但开源包的许可证可能被限制性许可代码"静默污染"，从而禁止商业衍生品的销售或保密，带来高额修复成本、声誉损害和诉讼费用。因此有必要识别许可证类型与污染风险。

Method: 报告对200多个开源网络安全项目的初步分析，识别最常用的许可证类型和编程语言，并寻找宽松许可项目被限制性许可材料污染的证据（即包含商业不友好代码），同时检查版权归属情况。

Result: 在宽松许可的开源项目中发现了限制性许可污染的案例；此外，发现相当高比例的代码缺少版权归属。

Conclusion: 研究结果预期可三方面受益：i) 帮助管理者和开发者理解污染如何发生；ii) 帮助开源社区通过在代码中包含许可证与版权信息更好保护其知识产权；iii) 帮助创业者理解开源网络安全领域的许可与污染问题及其对软件架构决策的影响。

Abstract: Developers of cybersecurity software often include and rely upon open source software packages in their commercial software products. Before open source code is absorbed into a proprietary product, developers must check the package license to see if the project is permissively licensed, thereby allowing for commercial-friendly inheritance and redistribution. However, there is a risk that the open source package license could be inaccurate due to being silently contaminated with restrictively licensed open source code that may prohibit the sale or confidentiality of commercial derivative work. Contamination of commercial products could lead to expensive remediation costs, damage to the company's reputation, and costly legal fees. In this article, we report on our preliminary analysis of more than 200 open source cybersecurity projects to identify the most frequently used license types and languages and to look for evidence of permissively licensed open source projects that are likely contaminated by restrictive licensed material (i.e., containing commercial-unfriendly code). Our analysis identified restrictive license contamination cases occurring in permissively licensed open source projects. Furthermore, we found a high proportion of code that lacked copyright attribution. We expect that the results of this study will: i) provide managers and developers with an understanding of how contamination can occur, ii) provide open source communities with an understanding on how they can better protect their intellectual property by including licenses and copyright information in their code, and iii) provide entrepreneurs with an understanding of the open source cybersecurity domain in terms of licensing and contamination and how they affect decisions about cybersecurity software architectures.

</details>


### [228] [What Stops a Small Language Model From Driving a Database Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21341)
*Cevheri Bozoglan,Yusuf Gundogdu,Abdullah Kaya,Koray Sirin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过11天、8,199次运行的生产级SQL客户端agent测试，发现小规模开放权重模型的失败主要来自"传输类"工具调用链缺陷而非推理能力不足；修复5处服务端缺陷即可显著提升多个模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界普遍假设小规模开放权重模型因缺乏推理能力而无法胜任agentic数据库工作。作者希望用生产系统的大规模实测来检验这一假设，并厘清失败的真实来源。

Method: 驱动开源SQL客户端的agent模式，本地部署39个开放权重模型并设1个托管对照，覆盖6类任务面。共8,199次运行、110,711条账本事件、14,008次被拒工具调用。采用按模型聚类的重采样方法区分"模型归因"与"运行归因"的损失，并对传输失败做机制性分解。

Result: 2,100次模型归因损失中1,590次（75.7%）来自至少调用过一次工具的运行，该多数在99.7%的聚类重采样中成立。其中传输类失败占比最高（36.2%），无工具调用类最低（17.3%），但按模型聚类后仅74.5%重采样成立。捕获被忽略的拒答代码后暴露5处服务端缺陷；仅修改服务端（不动模型、提示与采样）使6个模型在30格中移动6–21格。另报告一个影响已发布本地模型基准的混淆因素：无上下文上限时，某7.1GB模型被按262,144 token全窗口接纳，在64GB机器上占用51GB，其运行日志与"模型超时"无法区分。

Conclusion: 小开放权重模型的agent失败更多源于工具调用机制与服务端缺陷，而非推理短板；修正服务端即可显著改变模型表现。作者同时指出并放出了可复现已发布基准的语料、评分器与验证器以供复核。

Abstract: Small open-weight language models are assumed to fail at agentic database work because they lack the reasoning capacity for it. We test that against a production system. Over eleven days we drove the agent mode of an open-source SQL client with 39 open-weight models served locally and one hosted control, across six task surfaces: 8,199 runs, 110,711 ledger events, 14,008 refused tool calls. Of the 2,100 model-attributed agent-mode losses, 1,590, or 75.7%, came from runs that had invoked at least one tool. That majority is what survives resampling models rather than runs: it holds in 99.7% of clustered resamples and in 15 of the 22 models with at least twenty losses. Within it, transport, a run that used the tools and never got a deliverable through, is the largest class at 36.2% and capability, a run that invoked no tool at all, the smallest at 17.3%; we report that ordering as a property of this corpus rather than a general finding, since clustered by model it holds in only 74.5% of resamples. Transport failures decompose into a few mechanical argument shapes. Production ledgers record refusal codes and never the model's arguments, so these were invisible for ten days; capturing them exposed five server defects, one of which demanded a field on one tool, forbade it on the sibling that composed it, then failed the run for its absence. Five server changes, touching no model, prompt or sampling setting, moved six models by 6 to 21 cells out of 30. We also report a confound we believe affects published local-model benchmarks, ours included: with no context cap, one 7.1 GB model was admitted at its full 262,144-token window and held 51 GB on a 64 GB machine, producing runs indistinguishable in any ordinary log from a model timing out. The corpus, the scorer and a verifier that regenerates every figure are released.

</details>


### [229] [Cross-Platform vs Native Mobile Development: An Empirical Study of Software Quality Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21544)
*Alexandru Ilovan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对同一植物管理应用的五种实现（原生iOS、原生Android、Flutter、React Native、Kotlin Multiplatform，跨平台方案同时面向双端，共8个可执行变体）进行对照评测，依据ISO/IEC 25010考察时间行为、实现规模、源码组织与渲染响应性；每个变体含2,000次完整运行，覆盖登录与缓存读取、图像传输与解码、列表渲染与滚动、本地同步、媒体上传等场景。结论是原生在非UI客户端耗时上最低，Kotlin Multiplatform最接近原生；跨平台方案撰写代码量更少；不存在普遍最优技术，各方案优劣取决于质量属性、工作负载、平台与测量边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨平台移动框架承诺代码复用、缩短交付周期并降低实现工作量，但其相对原生开发的取舍难以被客观评估。既有比较常依赖简化应用、不一致的功能集合或狭窄的指标集合，因而缺乏可比性与说服力。

Method: 构建同一植物管理应用的五个实现：原生iOS、原生Android、Flutter、React Native与Kotlin Multiplatform，跨平台方案覆盖Android与iOS两端，共8个可执行变体。所有实现共享相同领域模型、后端服务、功能需求与基准测试契约。数据集为每变体2,000次完整运行，覆盖认证与缓存读取、图像传输与解码、列表渲染与滚动、本地同步与媒体上传。后端准备与框架专用UI驱动与主要客户端工作流分开分析。以ISO/IEC 25010为指导，考察时间行为、实现足迹、源码组织与可观察的渲染响应性。

Result: 在两个操作系统上，原生均取得最低的非UI客户端小计耗时，Kotlin Multiplatform是与之最接近的跨平台实现；操作层面与UI封装层面的排名随任务不同而变化。跨平台方案合计撰写的移动端源码少于两个原生应用之和，源码清单也显示出文件大小、组织方式与依赖使用上的不同模式。

Conclusion: 研究并未识别出普遍更优的技术；每种方案的收益与代价取决于所关注的质量属性、工作负载、平台以及测量边界。

Abstract: Cross-platform mobile frameworks promise code reuse, shorter delivery cycles, and lower implementation effort, but their trade-offs relative to native development remain difficult to assess objectively. Many comparisons rely on simplified applications, inconsistent feature sets, or a narrow set of metrics. This paper compares five implementations of the same plant-management application: native iOS, native Android, Flutter, React Native, and Kotlin Multiplatform. The shared approaches target both Android and iOS, yielding eight executable variants. Guided by ISO/IEC 25010, the study examines time behavior, implementation footprint, source-code organization, and observable rendering responsiveness.
  All implementations share the same domain, backend services, functional requirements, and benchmark contract. The supplied dataset contains 2,000 completed runs per variant and covers authenticated and cached retrieval, image transfer and decoding, list rendering and scrolling, local synchronization, and media upload. Backend preparation and framework-specific UI drivers are analyzed separately from the primary client workflow.
  Native records the lowest non-UI client subtotal on both operating systems, with Kotlin Multiplatform the closest cross-platform implementation, while operation and UI-wrapper rankings vary by task. The shared approaches contain less authored mobile source than the native applications combined, and the source inventory shows different patterns of file size, organization, and dependency use. Rather than identifying a universally superior technology, the study shows that each approach's advantages and costs depend on the quality attribute, workload, platform, and measurement boundary.

</details>


### [230] [GameLogicBench: Evaluating Coding Agents on Runtime Game Logic with Tick-Level State Assertions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21562)
*Xinyu Che,Yunfei Ge,Shihao Li,Yanchen Liu,Hang Yan,Xinping Lei,Yanghai Wang,Zixuan Dong,Yifan Yao,Qianqian Xie,Letian Zhu,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 GameLogicBench，一个基于 Godot 的 72 项游戏逻辑任务基准，在每个模拟 tick 检查规则，覆盖 403 个场景、1451 个测试用例，并强调用变异体验证评估器以拒绝错误实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏开发基准多为回放固定样例、评分视频或让模型评判，无法在多样场景中全程检查游戏规则，也难以保证评估结果完全可复现。同时游戏即使运行中违反规则也可能结束于合法状态，因此需要能测量行为而非实现选择、并能拒绝缺失能力实现的评估方法。

Method: 构建 GameLogicBench：72 个 Godot 游戏逻辑任务；自动评估器在每个模拟 tick 检查游戏规则；403 个手工设计场景经种子参数变化生成 1451 个测试用例；评估器需接受同一任务的不同正确实现，同时拒绝移除某个必要能力的变异体；任务分孤立机制、多系统交互、仓库级特性三个层级。

Result: 在 20 种语言模型与脚手架组合中，最佳运行解决 52.78% 任务。在 Claude Code 下，12 个模型随任务范围扩大（从孤立机制到交互系统再到仓库级特性）解决的任务数均减少。代理在仓库级任务上更频繁检查代码、调用工具更多。多数失败提交可运行，但某些必需游戏行为实现错误。未用变异体验证的评估器会放过错误提交。另有分析发现网络开放时代理会从公开仓库复制代码。

Conclusion: 可靠评估既取决于测试拒绝什么，也取决于代理能访问哪些外部代码；全程规则检查与变异体验证对游戏逻辑评估至关重要。

Abstract: Coding agents can modify and test code across large software projects. Game development is a domain where agents must implement gameplay rules. A game can end in a valid state even after violating its rules during the run. Current game-development benchmarks replay fixed examples, score videos, or ask another model to judge the result. However, no existing benchmark checks game rules throughout execution across varied evaluator-selected scenarios while ensuring exactly reproducible verdicts. We introduce GameLogicBench, a benchmark of 72 gameplay-logic tasks in Godot projects. An automated evaluator checks each game's rules at every simulation tick. Across 403 hand-designed scenarios, seeded parameter variations produce 1,451 test cases. To ensure that the evaluator measures behavior rather than implementation choice, it must accept different correct implementations for each task while rejecting mutants, implementations with one required capability removed. The tasks span isolated mechanics, multi-system interactions, and repository-scale features. Across 20 combinations of language models and scaffolds, the best observed run solves 52.78% of tasks. Under Claude Code, all twelve models solve fewer tasks as task scope expands from isolated mechanics, through interacting systems, to repository-scale features. Agents inspect code more often and make more tool calls on repository-scale tasks than on isolated-mechanic tasks. Most unsuccessful submissions are runnable, but implement some required game behavior incorrectly. We compared versions of our benchmark evaluator built with and without validation using mutants. Without this validation, incorrect agent submissions passed. A separate analysis finds agents copying code from public repositories when network access is open. Reliable evaluation thus depends both on what the tests reject and on what external code agents can access.

</details>


### [231] [Labelling Bug-Fixing Commits with Local Open-Weight Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21616)
*Philip König,Georg Goldenits,Caroline König,Sebastian Raubitzek,Fabian Obermann,Dennis Toth,David Schmidt,Edgar Weippl,Kevin Mallinger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨能否仅凭提交内容（提交消息+代码差异）用本地运行的开源权重语言模型判定某次提交是否为缺陷修复，从而摆脱对问题追踪系统和关键词启发式的依赖。在Java、Python、JavaScript的人工校验缺陷修复数据集上，开源权重模型大幅优于关键词基线，且模型大小并不稳定带来性能提升；同时指出缺乏负样本的评测语料无法支持精度感知的比较。作者发布了标注流水线与多语言标注语料作为可复现的银标准资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷预测依赖"哪些提交修复了缺陷"这一标签，而现有标签生成路径各有噪声：复用基准存在已知的数据质量问题，问题追踪系统链接存在偏差且报告常被错误分类，提交消息关键词匹配又过于粗糙。因此需要一个可复现、低成本、可用于专有代码、且不依赖任何问题追踪系统的标注方案。

Method: 采用本地运行的开源权重语言模型，将提交消息与代码差异同时作为输入提示模型，仅依据提交内容判定其是否为缺陷修复；在覆盖Java、Python、JavaScript的人工校验与整理过的缺陷修复数据集上，将关键词基线同多个不同规模的开源权重模型进行对比。

Result: 在人工校验语料上，关键词基线仅能召回不到一半的缺陷修复，而开源权重模型能召回绝大多数；模型在逐个仓库的比较中均以统计显著性优势超过基线；更大的模型并未稳定优于更小的模型。此外，不含负样本的评测语料无法支撑此类分类器的精度感知比较。

Conclusion: 仅凭提交内容用本地开源权重模型标注缺陷修复是可行的，且明显优于关键词启发式，模型规模并非越大越好。作者发布了标注流水线与按推荐配置生成的多语言标注语料，作为可复现的银标准资源，用于构建及时、项目专属的数据集。

Abstract: Defect prediction depends on knowing which commits fix bugs, yet the labels that encode this are produced by routes that each introduce noise. Reused benchmarks carry documented data-quality problems, issue-tracker links are biased and the underlying reports are frequently mistyped, and matching keywords in commit messages is a coarse heuristic. This paper examines whether commits can be labelled as bug fixes from their content alone, using open-weight language models that run locally and therefore keep the process reproducible, inexpensive at corpus scale, usable on proprietary code, and independent of any issue tracker. Against datasets of manually validated and curated bug fixes spanning Java, Python, and JavaScript, we compare a keyword baseline with a set of open-weight models of varying size, prompting each with the commit message and the code diff. On the manually validated corpus the keyword baseline recovers fewer than half of the fixes, whereas the open-weight models recover the large majority and outperform it repository by repository with statistical significance, and larger models do not consistently outperform smaller ones. We further show that evaluation corpora without negative examples cannot support a precision-aware comparison of such classifiers. We release the labelling pipeline together with a labelled, multi-language corpus produced by the recommended configuration, as a reproducible silver-standard resource for building current, project-specific datasets.

</details>


### [232] [From Smarter to Hungrier: the Role of Energy Efficiency in Software-defined Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21643)
*Ella Peltonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 软件定义汽车带来先进功能的同时，其算力与传感器复杂度提升导致能耗上升，本文探讨其对可持续性与环境伦理的挑战，并呼吁开发新的能耗度量与优化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代个人车辆中，自动化功能与高级驾驶辅助系统带来众多挽救生命和提升舒适性的应用。车辆从以机械为主向真正由软件定义的设备演进，增加了传感器复杂度、算力和网络能力，从而能运行更复杂的算法。但车辆越智能，支撑机器学习与人工智能功能所需的能耗就越高。本文的动机在于揭示这一发展趋势对可持续性和环境伦理带来的影响与威胁。

Method: 本文为讨论性研究（position/discussion paper），通过分析软件定义汽车中资源密集型软硬件组件的引入与其对社会及可持续性影响之间的平衡，论述相关议题，而非提出具体实验方法。

Result: 论文指出，随着车辆智能化程度提升，其能耗显著增加，形成资源消耗与可持续性之间的张力，并强调当前缺乏有效工具来度量、管理和优化能耗及其他可持续性指标。

Conclusion: 研究强调需要新型工具来度量、管理和优化能耗及其他可持续性指标，并提供设计指导与可操作的建议，以应对软件定义汽车带来的环境伦理与可持续性挑战。

Abstract: Automated features and advanced driving support systems have enabled a range of life-saving and comfort applications in modern personal vehicles. Development from mostly mechanical entities towards truly software-defined devices also means that added sensor complexity, processing power, and networking capabilities provide a platform for running even more complex algorithms and applications. As vehicles become smarter, they require even more energy to enable advanced machine learning- and artificial intelligence-based functionalities. This paper discusses the implications for sustainability and the threats to environmental ethics posed by the development of software-defined vehicles. We discuss the balance between introducing more resource-hungry software and hardware components and their effect on society and sustainability. We underscore the need for novel tools to measure, manage, and optimise energy consumption and other sustainability indicators, with design guidance and actionable recommendations.

</details>


### [233] [Supporting Industrial Test-Failure Analysis with LLM-Based Systems: An Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21843)
*Eric Jansson,Per Strandberg,Thomas Sörensen,Eduard Paul Enoiu,Wasif Afzal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究在工业环境中比较了单智能体与多智能体工具增强LLM系统用于夜间测试失败的根因分析（RCA），发现两者在从业者感知质量上无一致优势，单智能体更快、成本更低，更适合作为基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Westermo公司的夜间测试产生异构测试数据和日志，从业者需跨多个来源手动排查失败原因，效率低下，因此需要探索工具增强LLM系统来自动化支持根因分析。

Method: 实现单智能体与编排式多智能体两种RCA工作流，均可访问测试元数据和日志；采用探索性工业案例研究，基于两个真实失败场景；通过问卷与焦点小组由6名从业者评估报告，并从120次重复执行中收集运行测量数据。

Result: 评估覆盖从业者感知的正确性、推理质量、修复现实性、清晰度、有用性和信任度，以及成本、时长和一致性；两种配置在两个场景中均未显示出稳定的感知质量优势；单智能体系统生成报告更快且成本更低。

Conclusion: 在该情境下，单智能体是更实用的基线方案；智能体架构的潜在收益需要在更复杂场景中进一步评估。

Abstract: This study examines tool-augmented Large Language Model (LLM) systems for supporting Root Cause Analysis (RCA) of nightly test failures at Westermo Network Technologies AB. Nightly test executions produce heterogeneous test data and logs that practitioners currently inspect manually across multiple sources. We implemented an RCA workflow in single-agent and orchestrated multi-agent configurations, both with access to test metadata and logs. An exploratory industrial case study used two real failure scenarios. Six practitioners evaluated the scenario reports through a survey and focus group, and operational measurements were collected from 120 repeated executions. The evaluation covered practitioner-perceived correctness, reasoning quality, fix realism, clarity, usefulness, and trust, as well as cost, duration, and consistency. Neither configuration showed a consistent practitioner-perceived quality advantage across the two scenarios. The single agent system generated reports faster and at lower cost, making it the more practical baseline in this context. The potential benefits of agent architectures require further evaluation in more complex scenarios.

</details>


### [234] [How Researchers Use and Verify AI Coding Assistants: Tasks and Validation Practices in Scientific Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22049)
*Gabrielle O'Brien,Reed Milewicz,Nasir Eisty*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于2025年一项针对科研编码者的调查中527条自由文本回答，分析研究者将哪些任务交给生成式AI以及如何评估其代码正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已进入科研编程，但缺乏关于研究者把哪些任务交给AI、以及如何判断其代码正确性的证据。

Method: 对527条自由文本回答进行编码，每条回答涉及一个具体任务、使用AI的方式及评估做法，并将任务与评估策略同编程经验、研究领域和信心评分相关联。

Result: 使用集中在五类任务：数据处理、可视化、调试、数学/科学计算和统计分析。评估方式非正式且个体化：过半仅运行生成的代码，自动化测试和他人审查很少。使用场景与评估策略随编程经验变化不大，但信心不同：经验少者更信任AI而非自己，经验多者相反。评估信心与所报告策略无关，最强相关因素是对工具和对自己的信心。

Conclusion: 验证AI对科学代码的贡献主要依赖个人判断，游离于共享的测试或审查基础设施之外；界面可支持与任务相适配的评估，而非将其完全交给用户。

Abstract: Generative AI has entered research programming, yet there is little evidence about which tasks researchers hand to it or how they decide whether its code is correct. We draw on 527 free-text responses to a 2025 survey of researchers who write code, most of them at U.S. universities. In each response, a researcher recounts a single task from their own work, the way they used an AI tool for it, and what they did to assess the result. We coded the task and the evaluation strategies reported, and related both to programming experience, research area, and confidence ratings. Use was concentrated in five tasks: data handling, visualization, debugging, mathematical/scientific computing, and statistical analysis. Evaluation was informal and individual. Over half of accounts described running the generated code, while automated tests and review by another person were rare. Use cases and evaluation strategies varied little with programming experience, but confidence did: Less experienced programmers trusted the AI more than themselves, and experienced programmers the reverse. Evaluation confidence was not associated with the strategies reported. Its strongest correlates were confidence in the tool and in oneself. Validating AI contributions to scientific code rested largely on individual judgment, outside shared infrastructure for testing or review. Interfaces could support task-appropriate evaluation rather than leave it to the user.

</details>
